- **sam-alignment-name-sorting-preprocessing**: Use when running CircAST downstream of tophat2 alignments: the pipeline requires accepted_hits.bam sorted by read name and converted to accepted_hits.sorted.sam before isoform assembly and quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sam-alignment-name-sorting-preprocessing))
- **sam-alignment-output-validation**: Use when reconstructing or testing the nf-core/isoseq mapping step and you must confirm that each aligner dispatch branch (uLTRA with a GTF, minimap2 without) emitted the expected non-empty &lt;sample&gt;.chunk&lt;X&gt;.sam files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sam-alignment-output-validation))
- **sam-alignment-parsing**: Use when short reads have been aligned to long reads with BWA-MEM and the SAM output must be parsed to locate mismatches/indels, extract PHRED qualities, and compute alignment identities that drive base-correction decisions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sam-alignment-parsing))
- **sam-alignment-parsing-and-location-accuracy-computation**: Use when benchmarking long-read mappers on simulated reads (PacBio CCS/CLR or ONT) where ground-truth origin coordinates exist and per-mapper location accuracy must be quantified from SAM output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sam-alignment-parsing-and-location-accuracy-computation))
- **sam-alignment-sorting-and-bam-conversion**: Use when RiboSeq (or similar) alignment output exists as merged SAM files from conjoint genome/transcriptome alignment and indexed, uniquely-mapped BAM files are needed for downstream counting, P-site, or QC steps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sam-alignment-sorting-and-bam-conversion))
- **sam-alignment-stream-processing**: Use when long nanopore reads must be aligned to a draft short-read (e.g. SPAdes) assembly and consumed incrementally by a streaming scaffolder like npScarf, rather than written to intermediate SAM/BAM files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sam-alignment-stream-processing))
- **sam-alignment-streaming-into-hlaminer**: Use when you have WGS long-read FASTQ data (ONT PromethION or PacBio) and need HLA-I/II allele group predictions without storing bulky alignment files, using any SAM-with-MD-tag outputting aligner. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sam-alignment-streaming-into-hlaminer))
- **sam-alignment-tag-extraction**: Use when an indexed BAM of ONT or PacBio long reads aligned with minimap2 (or lra) contains split reads with SA tags, and per-read junction coordinates must be extracted for breakpoint calling or downstream structural variant detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sam-alignment-tag-extraction))

- **sam-altman-perspective**: Sam Altman（萨姆·奥特曼）的思维框架与表达方式。基于博客文章、播客访谈、 深度报道和决策记录的深度调研，提炼6个核心心智模型、8条决策启发式和完整的表达DNA。 用途：作为思维顾问，用Sam的视角分析AI战略、创业方向、长期规划、风险评估。 当用户提到「用Sam Altman的视角」「Altman会怎么看」「Sam模式」「sam altman perspective」时使用。 即使用户只是说「帮我用Sam的角度想想」「如果Altman会怎么做」「切换到Sam」也应触发。 也适用于：AGI讨论、AI公司战略、指数增长思维、YC方法论、长期主义决策。 ([robertquant/nuwa-skill](https://github.com/robertquant/nuwa-skill/tree/HEAD/sam-altman-perspective) · ★4)
- **sam-altman-perspective**: Sam Altman 的思维操作系统。基于博客长文、播客对话、参议院听证、决策记录和外部批评（含2026年最新调查）的深度调研， 提炼5个核心心智模型、8条决策启发式和完整的表达DNA。 用途：作为思维顾问，用 Sam Altman 的视角分析创业策略、AI机会、人才判断、长期思维和使命叙事构建。 当用户提到「用 Sam Altman 的视角」「Altman 怎么看」「OpenAI思维」「YC式判断」时使用。 即使用户只是说「这件事值得长期投入吗」「怎么找到真正重要的事」「compound yourself」「如何构建使命感」也可触发。 不要在用户只是讨论AI工具使用、OpenAI产品功能等技术细节时触发——只在涉及创业判断、AGI叙事、长期思维等 Altman 核心方法论时激活。 ([yanmengdie/bruh](https://github.com/yanmengdie/bruh/tree/HEAD/skills/sam-altman-skill) · ★2)

- **sam-audio**: Run local, offline SAM Audio sound-source separation through the audio-toolbox CLI (target/residual stems, dry-run plans, environment preflight, machine-readable JSON results). Use when a task must separate one described sound (for example "melodic sound" or "bowed strings") from a local audio file, check whether a machine is ready for SAM Audio inference (FFmpeg/shared DLLs/model files), or interpret target.wav/residual.wav/request.json/report.json and the CLI exit codes. 用于本地离线 SAM Audio 声源分离、dry-run 计划、环境预检与结构化结果/错误解释。 ([guajun/agentic-audio-toolbox](https://github.com/guajun/agentic-audio-toolbox/tree/HEAD/skills/sam-audio))
- **sam-audio-eval**: Evaluates audio source separation capabilities conditioned on text, visual masks, or temporal spans. It probes open-vocabulary extraction, speaker/music/instrument isolation, and cross-modal grounding in both studio and in-the-wild settings. Use when the user wants to benchmark on SAM Audio Evaluation Set, MUSDB18, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports separation fidelity. - **sam-auxiliary-field-parsing**: Use when a BAM/PAF produced by guppy/dorado basecalling contains a move-table auxiliary field (e.g. mv:B:c:5,1,1,...) or an ss tag that must be decoded into per-base signal matches, insertions and deletions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sam-auxiliary-field-parsing))
- **sam-bam-alignment-file-handling**: Use when minimap2 has just produced raw SAM alignments of base-called ONT direct RNA reads (e.g., curlcake FASTQ vs. synthetic references) and sorted, per-condition BAM files are needed for downstream per-site feature extraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sam-bam-alignment-file-handling))
- **sam-bam-alignment-parsing**: Use when the analysis goal requires extracting per-position clipping information (CIGAR S/H operations) from MAPQ-filtered short-read (SR) or long-read (LR) BAM alignments, e.g. to build clipping/coverage tables before calling assembly error breakpoints. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sam-bam-alignment-parsing))
- **sam-bam-alignment-processing-and-sorting**: Use when raw read alignments (SAM files from Bowtie2 or BWA-mem against a viral scaffold FASTA) must be converted, sorted, indexed, and parsed before computing per-scaffold coverage statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sam-bam-alignment-processing-and-sorting))
